UFOs Aliens And Now Photos Of The Dead


                                                          UFO Updates
                                   A mailing list for the study of UFO-related phenomena
                                               'Its All Here In Black & White'
    Location: UFOUpDatesList.Com > 2010 > Nov > Nov 21

                                          UFOs Aliens And Now Photos Of The Dead
  From: Dave Haith <visions1.nul>
  Date: Sun, 21 Nov 2010 09:42:12 -0000
  Archived: Sun, 21 Nov 2010 06:58:26 -0500
  Subject: UFOs Aliens And Now Photos Of The Dead

  I sent the below item round my personal eMail List because
  frankly, I'm fascinated with this subject.
  It may seem off-topic to be posting it here but as you all know
  this guy Robbert also claims to be creating - or channelling -
  UFO photos as well as images of grey aliens.
  And in many of these paranormal areas there's a definite
  crossover.
  Nancy Talbott is also no stranger to this List and has been
  interviewed on Strange Days... Indeed.
  -----
  Get ready for your "boggle" factor to be challenged people...
  For several years a crop circle/UFO researcher and friend Nancy
  Talbott has been visiting and studying medium Robbert van de
  Broeke in Holland.
  Her website has featured this young man allegedly capturing all
  manner of anomalous images in digital cameras ranging from light
  orbs and strange wispy mists to UFOs, alien faces and also
  pictures of people who have died.
  It's the latter group which makes up the bulk of her latest six
  page report on her website here: http://tiny.cc/cen2e
  Some of this work has appeared previously and has been attacked
  all over the internet by sceptics and debunkers who claim
  Robbert is a fraud and trickster.
  But Nancy has persevered undeterred because she is totally
  convinced - because she has been a prime witness along with
  others - that Robbert's strange talents are genuine.
  She claims that many photos that Robbert has produced of dead
  people - simply by firing off a camera often randomly - have
  been recognised by their living relatives.
  Nancy was even present when 60 photos of her brother's face
  appeared on the camera screen - two months after he died.
  In the report linked above she tells of a woman disturbed by a
  'presence' in a house, Robert visited there, took some photos
  which depicted the woman's neigbour who had died years
  previously. Later he captured on camera, images of the woman's
  dead mother.
  Nancy further relates the story of the 'ghost' that came
  a'knocking at the door of a room where she was with Robbert.
  They heard three taps and Robbert photographed the closed door.
  The face of a man appeared on the camera screen several times as
  he pressed the shutter button.
  In the report there is the story of a mystery soldier appearing
  on screen who was later identified to be almost exactly - but
  with subtle differences - the same as a soldier in the book of a


http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2010/nov/m21-008.shtml[12/12/2011 09:41:27]
UFOs Aliens And Now Photos Of The Dead

  military neigbour!
  Summing up what may be happening Nancy suggests Robbert may be
  "imbued with an expanded consciousness".
  She writes: "That fits most reasonably my many years of direct
  observation of these events. And that with this remarkable
  sensitivity comes his ability to not only perceive, but to
  interact with, what may very well be multiple currently unknown
  aspects of consciousness or some other as yet unknown forces of
  nature not evident to most of the rest of us."
  The report and pictures again here:
  http://www.bltresearch.com/robbert/apparition1.php
  -----
  Dave Haith

  evidence" is there for anyone who cares to listen.

  tapes do two things:

  2) Affirm the interviews took place and the dialogue was
  recounted accurately by Robert Hastings & Bob Salas.

  Greetings Dave, Everyone,
  The late Henry Gordon, a Canadian magician and co-founder of
  Ontario Skeptics, was invited to York University a few years ago
  to do a special magic show for our scientists and science
  students. If seeing is believing, then many of us that day would
  have joined the growing ranks of "experts" that have witnessed
  the impossible or paranormal. Unlike those who deceive us, Henry
  later showed us how he performed some of his amazing tricks. At
  the conclusion of his visit Henry told me that he enjoyed
  performing magic to scientists since he had more success in
  amazing them than school kids who were obviously more observant
  and better critical thinkers too...
  Mechanically, the human eye images objects very much the same
  way cameras (both still and moving pictures) do. It is able to
  selectively unscramble the electromagnetic mess all around us
  and clearly focus images of the real objects present that are
  producing or reflecting/scattering waves in the visible range of
  the electromagnetic spectrum. There are many recorded instances
  by people more credible than "medium" Robbert van de Broeke
  where the eye saw and the camera captured the same real things.
  These anomolous things not only exhibited the same shadows and
  lighting effects as the rest of objects in the picture - not as
  the similar still 2-dimensional Robbert van de Broeke images or
  the famous Cottingley Fairies that physician Sir Arthur Conan
  Doyle was convinced were real.
  The evidence that Nancy Talbott, a believing member of the
  uncritical and overly enthusiastic BLT Research Team, presents
  in support that Robbert van de Broeke's images could not have
  been faked is actually very damning evidence that they were
  faked! For example a cut-out picture will appear slightly
  distorted or out of proportion in exactly the same way Nancy


http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2010/nov/m21-011.shtml[12/12/2011 09:41:29]
Re: UFOs Aliens And Now Photos Of The Dead

  noted if it is even slightly rotated/twsited or not perfectly
  flat when placed in front of the camera lens!
  Anomolous images, allegedly of actual ETs or alien/strange
  surroundings not visible to the human eye (for the same reason
  TV signals are not) were obtained by Wilbert B. Smith working
  together with fellow scientists and engineers in the 1950s who
  were experimenting with a different kind of television based on
  alien EM radio technology...

  Nick Balaskas

  From: Gildas Bourdais <bourdais.gildas.nul>

  >>Since I don't know what motivates them, I can't say why we are
  >>seeing more of them now. Maybe it suits them now to be taken for
  >>ETs.
  >>I'm trying to come to grips with something that defies common
  >>sense. Stepping out of the box sometimes helps to make things
  >>make sense again.
  <snip>
  Don,
  Thank you for your serious arguments.
  This debate is interesting because William Treurniet is trying
  hard to exclude the idea of alien craft, but he has to use
  aguments which - in his own words - "defy common sense", and he
  fails.
  The idea that a very secret organization, having made these
  craft, would decide to show them in order to make us believe
  that they are alien, and so better hide their human origin,
  sounds really absurd to me.
  I know that there is, behind this, the conspirationist idea,
  expressed notably by Dr Steven Greer, that these staged, false
  UFOs will open the door to a declaration of war against them,
  and a take over by that "Cabal", etc.
  All this is mad science-fiction to me.
  It reminds me of the legend of the first flying saucers of 1947
  being in fact Nazi UFOs made secretly in underground bases,
  maybe under the ice cap of the South Pole!
  Not long ago, I met people in France who toyed with that - Denis
  Denocla, for one.

UFO Investigator Barbara Bartholic Dies At Age 71


                                                          UFO Updates
                                    A mailing list for the study of UFO-related phenomena
                                                'Its All Here In Black & White'
    Location: UFOUpDatesList.Com > 2010 > Nov > Nov 22

                                    UFO Investigator Barbara Bartholic Dies At Age 71
  From: UFO UpDates - Toronto <post.nul>
  Date: Mon, 22 Nov 2010 08:00:23 -0500
  Archived: Mon, 22 Nov 2010 08:00:23 -0500
  Subject: UFO Investigator Barbara Bartholic Dies At Age 71


  Source: Tulsa World, Oklahoma, USA
  http://tinyurl.com/274798o
  11/20/2010

  UFO Investigator Barbara Bartholic Dies At Age 71

  By Tim Stanley
  World Staff Writer
  Close encounters with Barbara Bartholic usually sparked the same
  reaction.
  "She always left you laughing. She had this marvelous sense of
  humor," her sister, Catha Block, said.
  "Barbara just had this way of making people feel important -
  like you were the only person in the world."
  One out-of-this-world topic was no laughing matter, though, to
  the Tulsa artist and art gallery owner.
  Bartholic had been fascinated for years by the possibility of
  extraterrestrial life, and in the 1970s, the subject consumed
  her.
  Bartholic's subsequent field research into UFOs led to writing
  and speaking engagements. It wasn't long before she became a
  respected UFO authority worldwide.
  One of the consultants on the movie "Close Encounters of the
  Third Kind," she later wrote about her experiences in the book
  "Barbara: The Story of a UFO Investigator."
  Barbara Simon Bartholic died Nov. 10 after suffering a stroke.
  She was 71.
  A service is set for 10 a.m. Saturday at Memorial Park Cemetery
  Chapel under the direction of Add'Vantage Funeral Service.
  "Barbara really perceived a world that's larger than the one we
  live in," Block said, adding that she was never embarrassed by
  her sister's devotion to a subject that often draws stares.
  "We were a free-thinking bunch and were open to the idea," she
  said.
  "And it was so fascinating. It was like going out of your
  universe into another one. If I was able, I'd love to carry on
  her work."
  Bartholic, who moved to Tulsa from St. Louis, Mo., as a child,
  started out in an entirely different line of work - just one of
  many over her life.


http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2010/nov/m22-005.shtml[12/12/2011 09:41:51]
UFO Investigator Barbara Bartholic Dies At Age 71


  Her mother, Tulsan Alice Simon, was a fashion model when she was
  younger. So was her mother before her.
  Bartholic carried on the family tradition, starting at age 17.
  Her modeling took her to Los Angeles and New York City, where
  she worked with well-known fashion designers.
  After she married Tulsa artist Bob Bartholic, she decided to
  move on.
  A painter herself who excelled in oils, she and Bob became
  leading figures in the Tulsa art scene and ran two downtown art
  galleries in the 1970s.
  Barbara Bartholic also hosted her own television show, "Arts
  Arena," on a local cable channel. She interviewed Tulsa-area
  artists, but it wasn't a conventional talk show, her sister
  said.
  "She was very inventive and avant-garde," Block said.
  Bartholic's other interests began to take a back seat to her
  obsession with UFOs.
  For several years, she worked closely with friend and fellow UFO
  investigator Jacque F. Vallee, a French astronomer who inspired
  the "Close Encounters" character of Claude Lacombe, played by
  French film director Francois Truffaut.
  Bartholic interviewed alleged alien abductees and looked into
  reported sightings of UFOs around the country. After nearly 40
  years, she was convinced, her sister says, that we are not
  alone.
  Block added that Bartholic had been working recently on a
  documentary film on the subject.
  She is survived by four children, Braden Bartholic, Mandy
  Wilson, Catha Clark and Becky Bartholic; her mother, Alice
  Simon; one sister, Catha Block; six grandchildren; and one
  great-granddaughter.

http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2010/nov/m22-009.shtml[12/12/2011 09:41:53]
Re: Black Triangle Spotted Over Sutton

  >being in fact Nazi UFOs made secretly in underground bases,
  >maybe under the ice cap of the South Pole!
  >Not long ago, I met people in France who toyed with that - Denis
  >Denocla, for one.
  >You cannot kill that sort of myth. It's too exciting!
  As a person who has seen these triangular craft very up-close on
  numerous occasions while I lived in the Hudson Valley and my
  sightings were along with dozens of other people and thousands
  at two sightings, I can say emphatically that there was nothing
  about these craft that suggested anything extraterrestrial.
  Why would ETs need girder undercarriages? Why would they need
  afterburners? Navigational lights?
  Albeit the craft had no insignia but there were a few incidents
  which I'll hold to myself for now that clearly, irrefutably show
  they were occupied by Americans it says to me either the U.S.
  built them or that the U.S. has something to do with them.
  I did run into a gentleman who is one of the senior engineers on
  our aircraft. He had a very disturbing experience regarding
  these craft and he told me in no uncertain terms that if he
  didn't design or engineer a craft, we the U.S. do not have it. I
  was surprised because at that time he was living in my old
  neighborhood in the Hudson Valley and worked with friends and
  relatives at IBM.
  The people who were with me during the sightings were civilian,
  military, press, elected officials, scientists of the highest
  caliber, cops in abundance, and so many college professors it
  isn't even funny.
  Only on one occasion were two different craft observed. One very
  large circular craft that appeared to have been chased away by
  one of the smaller delta winged craft. That delta winged craft
  actually was hovering right over our heads and we didn't notice
  it until we heard some metal clanking noise and then the blue
  afterburners kicked in and the object shot off at high speed. We
  could clearly see it's outline and surface as amazingly the
  afterburners fired up and the craft was stationary for a few
  seconds before it tore ass west and then south west making a
  banking turn right over the city.
  So we have a conflict here and arguments aplenty. I know what I
  saw, I'm in contact with many of the people who were there with
  me and those in the know, one of which is willing to come
  forward have no doubt they're U.S. craft. How they would come to
  this conclusion is because they were briefed as members of the
  press regarding them. That info I can't publish here.
  So the debate rages and sorry if some people who boast knowledge
  of such issues haven't been informed but I can see why you
  haven't.
  Black triangles, boomerangs, we lived it. Seen them, touched
  them, photographed, video taped, avoided being run off the road
  and more.
  So all I know from actually being at the scene is that there
  were no indications these craft are of ET design. Might be.
  There are many other craft, saucers especially seen in the
  region for decades by highly credible witnesses. The public is
  going to have to wait.

  Best,
  Greg

  >This debate is interesting because William Treurniet is trying
  >hard to exclude the idea of alien craft, but he has to use
  >aguments which - in his own words - "defy common sense" and he
  >fails.
  Please, Gildas, try to not misrepresent my words. It's the so-
  called risk-taking by the triangle craft that defies common
  sense. My arguments are intended to be a step towards resolving
  the conundrum.
  As I've said several times before, I definitely do not exclude
  the idea of alien craft. I'm just questioning that all triangle
  craft are alien.
  >The idea that a very secret organization, having made these
  >craft, would decide to show them in order to make us believe
  >that they are alien, and so better hide their human origin,
  >sounds really absurd to me.
  The purpose of showing themselves is not to better hide their
  origin. The purpose is to fool people into thinking they are
  alien. When that belief is established, it can be used to extort
  money to build better human defenses against a non-existent
  enemy. I outlined the proposed scenario more fully in a response
  to Don Ledger.
  >I know that there is, behind this, the conspirationist idea,
  >expressed notably by Dr Steven Greer, that these staged, false
  >UFOs will open the door to a declaration of war against them,
  >and a take over by that "Cabal", etc.
  >All this is mad science-fiction to me.
  People should ask themselves how they would react today if a
  large triangle craft were to destroy the American white house
  building with a directed energy weapon? Would you immediately
  assume that the attacker was alien, or would you consider the
  possibility that some earthlings may have developed the
  capability to carry out this attack?
  My guess is that many people, especially if encouraged by a
  media propaganda machine, would assume that the culprits were


http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2010/nov/m23-003.shtml[12/12/2011 09:42:09]
Re: Black Triangle Spotted Over Sutton

  alien. Therein lies the danger to society as a whole as I see
  it. We need to correct this imbalance by encouraging people to
  recognize that the triangle craft could be human-made, and they
  should wait for evidence before deciding who is the real enemy.
  If the triangle craft really are alien, then that would be
  confirmed soon enough. If they are human-made but kept secret,
  then there actually is a conspiracy afoot and we would be better
  prepared to deal with it.
  >It reminds me of the legend of the first flying saucers of 1947
  >being in fact Nazi UFOs made secretly in underground bases,
  >maybe under the ice cap of the South Pole!
  >Not long ago, I met people in France who toyed with that - Denis
  >Denocla, for one.
  >You cannot kill that sort of myth. It's too exciting!
  The Nazi connection is not required to account for the triangle
  craft as human-made. On the other hand, it fits quite nicely
  into that scenario.
  I pursue this line of thought not because it's exciting, but
  because I think the well-being of society could depend on it.
  Remember the saying - "fool me once shame on you, fool me twice
  shame on me". Well, I think we have been fooled already a
  number of times. Shame on us!

  William

  Baloney, eh? Here's a CBS online news report on the statement by
  Rumsfeld.
  http://tinyurl.com/6f82l
  "According to some estimates we cannot track $2.3 trillion in
  transactions," Rumsfeld admitted.
  $2.3 trillion =97 that's $8,000 for every man, woman and child in
  America. To understand how the Pentagon can lose track of
  trillions, consider the case of one military accountant who
  tried to find out what happened to a mere $300 million. "We know
  it's gone. But we don't know what they spent it on," said Jim
  Minnery, Defense Finance and Accounting Service. ...
  Twenty years ago, Department of Defense Analyst Franklin C.
  Spinney made headlines exposing what he calls the "accounting
  games." He's still there, and although he does not speak for the
  Pentagon, he believes the problem has gotten worse.
  "Those numbers are pie in the sky. The books are cooked
  routinely year after year," he said.
  Another critic of Pentagon waste, Retired Vice Admiral Jack
  Shanahan, commanded the Navy's 2nd Fleet the first time Donald
  Rumsfeld served as Defense Secretary, in 1976.
  In his opinion, "With good financial oversight we could find $48
  billion in loose change in that building, without having to hit
  the taxpayers."

  The I Go Pogo website discusses the history of the phrase:
  "Walt Kelly first used the quote 'We Have Met The Enemy and He
  Is Us' on a poster for Earth Day in 1970. The poster is shown
  above. In 1971, he did a two panel version with Pogo and Porky
  in a trash filled swamp. This is the only example I know of with
  a balloon, indicating Pogo responding to Porky with 'YEP, SON,
  WE HAVE MET THE ENEMY AND HE IS US.'"
  http://www.igopogo.com/we_have_met.htm
  There is a further quote from Walt Kelly explaining it (sans
  aliens) at:
  http://www.igopogo.com/final_authority.htm

                                                        Death Of Greta Woodrew
  From: Rich Heiden <rwheidenu.nul>
  Date: Mon, 22 Nov 2010 16:24:58 -0800 (PST)
  Archived: Tue, 23 Nov 2010 06:08:46 -0500
  Subject: Death Of Greta Woodrew

  On Oct. 21 I got a postcard informing me of the death of Greta
  Woodrew Smolowe on Sep. 1. She was a contactee and healer, and
  author of On A Slide of Light (as Greta Woodrew). I was on the
  mailing list because for years I had gotten her newsletter.
  The return address on the card says Advance, N.C., which is near
  Winston-Salem. But I couldn't find an obituary on the Web sites
  of either of the two newspapers there (the Journal and the
  Chronicle).

  Rich Heiden

                                                Buchanan Bros Flying Saucer Song
  From: Greg Boone <Evolbaby.nul>
  Date: Tue, 23 Nov 2010 01:58:22 -0500 (EST)
  Archived: Tue, 23 Nov 2010 06:18:19 -0500
  Subject: Buchanan Bros Flying Saucer Song

  Leave it to Norio Hayakawa to find this gem I hadn't heard
  since I don't know when.
  http://www.youtube.com/watch?v=yrIcZjIvQM0
  It's the Buchanan Brothers with their hillbilly gospel song,
  When You See Those Flying Saucers.
  I've got so many kin from the hills that when you have a last
  name like mine, Boone, you get teased a lot as folks say we
  invented the hills.
  This song is a real treat and emphasizes just how much a part of
  our culture UFOs have become. From songs to books to major
  motion pictures, to radio and the web the impact just keeps
  rolling on.
  Best,
  Greg
  ufomafia.com/blog

  [Had Greg listened to Strange Days... Indeed, the podcasts used
   this song more than a few times... But, leave it to Greg & Norio...
   ebk]

Martin Shough's 'Return Of The Flying Saucers'


                                                          UFO Updates
                                    A mailing list for the study of UFO-related phenomena
                                                'Its All Here In Black & White'

                                       Martin Shough's 'Return Of The Flying Saucers'
  From: Greg Taylor <greg.nul>
  Date: Tue, 23 Nov 2010 22:04:09 +1000
  Archived: Tue, 23 Nov 2010 07:23:05 -0500
  Subject: Martin Shough's 'Return Of The Flying Saucers'

  Hi listers,
  One of the fine researchers present on this List, Martin Shough,
  was kind enough to write a piece for the latest instalment
  (Volume 5) of my Fortean anthology series 'Darklore'. The
  article, titled Return Of The Flying Saucers condenses some of
  his earlier work on the Kenneth Arnold sighting, attempting to
  determine exactly what Arnold originally reported and whether he
  should be considered a credible witness.
  For those who are interested in reading Martin's excellent
  article, I've made it available as a free sample on the Darklore
  website, downloadable in its original formatting as a PDF (along
  with another article by myself critiquing skeptic Martin
  Gardner, and one on decoding crop circles by Richard J.
  Andrews). You can download the sample article (and others from
  earlier releases) via the following link:
  http://darklore.dailygrail.com/sample.php
  Please note that the editor was responsible for the title of the
  article, so any critique of that should be directed at me. For
  any other queries or feedback, I'm sure Martin would be more
  than happy to engage in discussion of his article.

  Kind regards,
  Greg

An Unusual UFO Over Reading England


                                                          UFO Updates
                                   A mailing list for the study of UFO-related phenomena
                                               'Its All Here In Black & White'
    Location: UFOUpDatesList.Com > 2010 > Nov > Nov 23

                                            An Unusual UFO Over Reading England
  From: William Treurniet <wtreurniet.nul>
  Date: Tue, 23 Nov 2010 09:17:21 -0500
  Archived: Tue, 23 Nov 2010 09:29:51 -0500
  Subject: An Unusual UFO Over Reading England

  I was recently provided with a sequence of 14 high resolution
  photos of an unusual object moving across the sky over Reading,
  England. The photos were taken during a 95 second interval in
  the late afternoon of July 17, 2010. The object is discussed in
  the following article.
  http://www.treurniet.ca/Ufo/reading1.htm
  The pink and white object has an organic shape that changed
  often over the sequence of photographs. It appears similar to a
  UFO photographed over New York City three months later just
  before the Oct 13 flap.
  The shapes in the Reading photos are similar to many aerial
  forms also photographed over the UK by Anthony Woods. There is
  speculation that the objects may be biological in nature.

  William

                                             Re: Buchanan Bros Flying Saucer Song
  From: Jerome Clark <jkclark.nul>
  Date: Tue, 23 Nov 2010 09:42:48 -0600
  Archived: Tue, 23 Nov 2010 11:12:19 -0500
  Subject: Re: Buchanan Bros Flying Saucer Song

  >From: Greg Boone <Evolbaby.nul>
  >To: post.nul
  >Date: Tue, 23 Nov 2010 01:58:22 -0500 (EST)
  >Subject: Buchanan Bros Flying Saucer Song
  >Leave it to Norio Hayakawa to find this gem I hadn't heard
  >since I don't know when.
  >http://www.youtube.com/watch?v=yrIcZjIvQM0
  >It's the Buchanan Brothers with their hillbilly gospel song,
  >When You See Those Flying Saucers.
  >I've got so many kin from the hills that when you have a last
  >name like mine, Boone, you get teased a lot as folks say we
  >invented the hills.
  >This song is a real treat and emphasizes just how much a part of
  >our culture UFOs have become. From songs to books to major
  >motion pictures, to radio and the web the impact just keeps
  >rolling on.
  This song has long been known to those of us interested in early
  hillbilly music, and it has been discussed in UFO literature
  since the early 1980s. I first heard it on an album of brother
  duets, who flourished in the country music of the 1930s (Monroe
  Brothers, Delmore Brothers, Blue Sky Boys et al.), in the latter
  1970s.
  Written by hard-working hacks Charlie Green and Cy Coben, When
  You See Those Flying Saucers was released in 1947. Its existence
  is utterly predictable. As with the street ballads that preceded
  commercial country music, songwriters once exploited fads,
  controversies, sensational crimes and the like.
  Often enough, the results were heavily moralistic, often
  explicitly religious, warning listeners that God is watching
  their behavior and prepared to act. Like many comparable songs,
  Flying Saucers predicts an imminent Judgment Day.
  Many other UFO-themed songs followed in various pop-music
  genres. My own favorite is the obscure Judge Proctor's
  Windmill, about the fabled Martian airship crash in Aurora,
  Texas, in 1897, recorded by folksinger Tom Pacheco in the 1970s.

  Jerry Clark

  From: Gerald O'Connell <goc.nul>
  Date: Tue, 23 Nov 2010 17:33:18 +0000

  >From: William Treurniet <wtreurniet.nul>

  >Date: Tue, 23 Nov 2010 09:17:21 -0500
  >Subject: An Unusual UFO Over Reading England
  >I was recently provided with a sequence of 14 high resolution
  >photos of an unusual object moving across the sky over Reading,
  >England. The photos were taken during a 95 second interval in
  >the late afternoon of July 17, 2010. The object is discussed in
  >the following article.
  >http://www.treurniet.ca/Ufo/reading1.htm
  >The pink and white object has an organic shape that changed
  >often over the sequence of photographs. It appears similar to a
  >UFO photographed over New York City three months later just
  >before the Oct 13 flap.
  >The shapes in the Reading photos are similar to many aerial
  >forms also photographed over the UK by Anthony Woods. There is
  >speculation that the objects may be biological in nature.
  In each of the frames there is at least one clearly spherical
  shape. In most of the frames there are several spherical shapes.
  It looks to me as though it is most probably a small bunch of
  pink balloons, tied together with red ribbon and tumbling in the
  wind.
  Circumstantially, July 17th was a Saturday, the day when
  shopping precincts/malls and markets are most likely to have
  this kind of stuff around and available and also the most
  popular day for the kind of celebrations where this kind of
  stuff is frequently involved. I suspect that this particular UFO
  was heading slowly towards the North Sea much to the
  disappointment of a group of rather young bridesmaids.

  Gerald O'Connell
  http://www.onlyport.com

  >not perfectly flat when placed in front of the camera lens!
  >Nick Balaskas
  Hi Nick,
  Your explanation maybe more realistic than "ghosts", but it
  can't explain everything. I am also skeptical regarding of these
  photos, but at the same moment I can't see how they can be
  faked. So I don't have an explanation other than he is really
  taking photos of "something".
  For example this can't be explained as cut-out pictures:
  "In broad daylight - and with Dr. Roll and me watching - six
  images (taken consecutively over about a minute) appeared on
  Roll's camera which show at first a "smokey" substance in front
  of Robbert's face which then develops into a man's face
  superimposed over Robbert's."
  http://www.bltresearch.com/robbert/apparition5.php
  "Robbert regularly obtains these photos during "readings" with
  his clients (using his clients' cameras) when he becomes aware
  of the "presence" of one of these clients' deceased relatives -
  and the resulting photographic images were being regularly
  identified - by Robbert's clients - as images of the very
  individuals they had come to ask Robbert about.
  The images do not appear on the LED screens until Robbert
  actually depresses the "record" button;
  Regardless of the available ambient light, the flash never
  fires.
  ...many "UFO" images began to appear repeatedly as Robbert used
  several people's cameras throughout the summer - and in those
  cases (including the instances when Robbert was using my camera)
  the camera-owners and multiple other witnesses were standing
  near Robbert and watching him closely.
  To produce some of the photos you will need to use Photoshop or
  similar program. But in these cases photos has appeared at the
  moment of shooting.
  You can't explain these photos by cut-out pictures or anything
  placed in front of camera without flash:
  http://www.bltresearch.com/robbert/photoanoms4.php

  I believe that scientist must start to think the other way
  round.
  Everywhere there is matter photons travel slower.
  Matter excist out of particles which are slower then what is in
  space.
  The amount of matter defines the speed of the photons.
  I can see with this theory that the more mass an object posses
  the larger the 'low pressure area' is around that object. But
  this has nothing to do with the radiation of such an object.
  For a Black Hole the 'low pessure area' around that Black Hole
  is that big and that low near that Black Hole that a lot of
  photons can't get enough energy to bounce back into space.
  For me in that theory gravity is nothing more than the result of
  a colliding between matter and space (ether) in which around
  matter space (ether) is slower because space lost energy because
  of the colliding with that matter.
  Therefore matter must eat (inhale) energy from space and there
  should be a stream of energy flowing from space towards matter
  and we see this as gravity. Matter must therefore convert that
  energy into something else like radiation or wathever. But it is
  just a theory (and it gives me a headache).

  articles on UFOs in the clouds. Apparently, the photos weighed on

  seriously.

  > BUFORA?
  No.

  Yoko Ono is the one who originally said "Woman is the nigger of
  the world" in a 1969 interview. John Lennon used it as a song
  lyric in 1972.
  Some people quote Alice Walker as saying "Women are the niggers
  of the universe." But she's quoting Lennon in her essay
  "Everything is a human being," and extends the idea to say "But,
  in truth, Earth itself has become the nigger of the world."

http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2010/nov/m24-015.shtml[12/12/2011 09:42:24]
The Dead & UFOs A Connection?


                                                          UFO Updates
                                   A mailing list for the study of UFO-related phenomena
                                               'Its All Here In Black & White'
    Location: UFOUpDatesList.Com > 2010 > Nov > Nov 25

                                                 The Dead & UFOs A Connection?
  From: Dave Haith <visions1.nul>
  Date: Wed, 24 Nov 2010 19:19:10 -0000
  Archived: Thu, 25 Nov 2010 06:10:22 -0500
  Subject: The Dead & UFOs A Connection?

  With reference to this video:
  http://tiny.cc/b17g3
  Like many UFO researchers I have found our subject cannot be
  confined to nuts and bolts - not when the mysterious objects
  come and go like will o'the wisps and abductees report beings
  suddenly materialising inside locked homes abducting people
  through solid walls.
  True there may be a number of different phenomena operating here
  but it seemed to me logical to explore related psychic events
  where similar happenings appear to occur - materialisation of
  matter, psychokinesis, communication with alleged spirits etc.
  And that's where Tom Harrison comes in - a very dear and close
  friend of mine who died just a few weeks ago in his 90s.
  Tom was an ardent Spiritualist but the stories he told me were
  far more spectacular than mumbled ambiguous messages from dodgy
  mediums.
  =46rom April 1946 Tom and his then wife Doris were part of a
  unique home s=E9ance circle known as The Saturday Night Club, a
  small group of family, friends and occasional fortunate guests
  who witnessed wonders that Spiritualists of today can only dream
  about. From that time until the passing of his mother Minnie
  Harrison, who was an exceptionally gifted medium, Tom and his
  fellow circle members claimed they met and talked with literally
  hundreds of materialised spirit people, all completely visible
  in good red light in the small back room of a house in
  Middlesbrough, UK.
  In his own words Tom said: "They returned not as fleeting,
  passing visions in somebody's mind, not even as wispy,
  transparent ghosts or spectres. They returned in fully-
  functioning, warm, heart-beating physical bodies. They returned
  and spoke with the same-sounding voices you would recognise.
  They returned with the same laughter, the same personality; and
  as you thrilled to feel their arms embracing you, and even
  kisses from their lips - the same love."
  Now this whole concept of dead people materialising so you could
  touch and chat with them was pretty mind blowing and I would
  have probably dismissed it completely unless I read of similar
  phenomena from the likes of Oliver Lodge and Sir Arthur Conan
  Doyle in the annals of parapsychology.
  So I got to know Tom who showed me some time-exposure photos
  he'd taken and his hand written diary of each occasion these
  phenomena occurred - literally hundreds of times.
  He talked of 'apports', objects suddenly appearing in their room
  and flower blooms being teleported through the wall from a
  garden outside.
  When I challenged him about the conversations with the visitors
  - some recorded on reel to reel tape - how could he be sure his
  group weren't all hallucinating, he would relate how a doctor


http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2010/nov/m25-001.shtml[12/12/2011 09:42:24]
The Dead & UFOs A Connection?

  guest once took the pulse of a materialised spirit and told her
  'You'll live' and she replied 'Yes I am living', and how with
  scissors he was permitted to snip some ectoplasm, from which the
  bodies formed, and take it in a jar to his hospital to have it
  analysed.
  When I asked Tom how he really knew the form he embraced and
  conversed with, was his mother, he replied "You would know your
  own mother, wouldn't you?"
  In his latter years I played a small role in helping him write a
  book about his experiences and secured him an interview on the
  Jeff Rense Show.
  Now following his passing, I note that somebody has posted an
  hour long video of Tom telling in detail of his experiences. The
  link for Visitors from the Other Side is here:
  http://tiny.cc/b17g3
  It's a home made video so sometimes the sound is a little rough
  but you'll hear Tom and former members of his circle telling of
  their experiences. And like me you might wonder if it could be
  true...
  Like me also, you might conjecture, what could be the connection
  between these ethereal visitors and the ones we are more
  familiar with in UFO lore, who invade our homes unbidden?
  My view is that in the quantum non-linear world there may be
  myriad dimensions which sometimes for reasons we currently
  cannot imagine, can blur into our own reality - hence the
  bizarre happenings at the Skinwalker Ranch and possibly in Nancy
  Talbott's current research linking UFOs with the world of the
  dead.
  I can well imagine the nuts and bolts brigade will criticise me
  from straying into 'la la land' - but if that's where the
  evidence leads, so be it.

  Dave Haith

Re: Black Holes


                                                          UFO Updates
                                   A mailing list for the study of UFO-related phenomena
                                               'Its All Here In Black & White'
    Location: UFOUpDatesList.Com > 2010 > Nov > Nov 25

                                                               Re: Black Holes
  From: Ray Dickenson <r.dickenson.nul>
  Date: Thu, 25 Nov 2010 01:14:41 -0000
  Archived: Thu, 25 Nov 2010 06:17:30 -0500
  Subject: Re: Black Holes

  >From: Bert Reijersen van Buuren <bert.nul>
  >To: <post.nul>
  >Date: Wed, 24 Nov 2010 04:39:56 +0100
  >Subject: Re: Black Holes
  <snip>
  >... there should be a stream of energy flowing from space
  >towards matter and we see this as gravity. Matter must therefore
  >convert that energy into something else like radiation or
  >wathever. But it is just a theory (and it gives me a headache).
  Hello Bert,
  The question is much simpler for me, as I'm relying only on
  observation (and a small theoretical step which I regard as
  'obvious').
  First, theoretical 'black holes' ('infinite' mass/density +
  'event horizons') would, if they existed in the visible
  universe, be unmistakable. The dragging of background starlight
  (necessary if 'event horizons' existed) would make them into
  huge targets of light, each surrounded by a large area of
  blackness - no background stars / galaxies. We don't ever see
  those things, or anything remotely like them.
  Second the ubiquity of 'jets', which we _do_ see all around the
  universe, tells me that accreting masses will always jet, at a
  limit of angular momentum. Which is why we see jets on all
  scales, even within our own atmosphere ('blue jets'). What do
  you think causes the 'bars' or initial spiral outbursts of our
  type of galaxy?
  And the fact of 'jetting' tells me that no accreting mass can
  ever grow larger than its 'jetting limit'. Therefore there are
  no black holes.

  Cheers
  Ray D
  BTW - mainstream astro-phys is stuck in denial, still claiming
  that all 'violent' phenomena are due to 'explosions'. But
  'explosions' just don't fit the bill for most 'violent'
  phenomena above the stellar scale - including distant gamma-ray
  bursts, which we see because we happen to be in-line with a jet.
  That explains both their intensity and the puzzle of the
  apparent smallness of their originating bodies - which is
  (visibly) only the width of the jet.

  http://www.virtuallystrange.net/ufo/sdi/program/

  >>popular day for the kind of celebrations where this kind of

  >>disappointment of a group of rather young bridesmaids.
  >I appreciate your comments, Gerald. I forwarded them to the

http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2010/nov/m25-005.shtml[12/12/2011 09:42:26]
Re: An Unusual UFO Over Reading England

  I've looked at it again as you suggested and three things occur
  to me:
  1. The original, unmagnified, shot you publish looks very much
  like a cluster of balloons, much more so than the blurred blow-
  ups.
  2. You publish a picture of a 'real balloon' taken the same day
  - doesn't that make you think that there was some helium party
  balloon activity going on that day? Isn't that a strong clue?
  3. The zig-zag movement isn't too surprising. The overall
  direction suggests wind - the zig-zagging would be caused by
  relative movement of the balloons within the cluster according
  to how they were secured to each other, and also the changing
  'face' that they consequently offered the to the wind. I have
  seen airborne plastic bags behave in a similar manner on a
  number of occasions when at a similar altitude.
  --
  Gerald O'Connell
  http://www.onlyport.com

http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2010/nov/m25-006.shtml[12/12/2011 09:42:27]
Re: An Unusual UFO Over Reading England


  >>Please see the article again to read the arguments that it was
  >>not a set of balloons tumbling in the wind.
  >I've looked at it again as you suggested and three things occur
  >to me:
  >1. The original, unmagnified, shot you publish looks very much
  >like a cluster of balloons, much more so than the blurred blow-
  >ups.
  Yes, that one might, but only the first and the fifth blowups
  have the segmented look that might make you think of multiple
  balloons. Towards the end of the sequence, the object might be
  interpreted as just one or a pair of balloons. So somehow it
  went from four to maybe one or two balloons?
  >2. You publish a picture of a 'real balloon' taken the same day
  >- doesn't that make you think that there was some helium party
  >balloon activity going on that day? Isn't that a strong clue?
  Maybe, maybe not. There were different properties. The balloon
  was a different colour, had a long tail relative to the balloon
  size, and was said to disappear quickly. If it was lower, as it
  seems, then it was likely smaller.
  >3. The zig-zag movement isn't too surprising. The overall
  >direction suggests wind - the zig-zagging would be caused by
  >relative movement of the balloons within the cluster according
  >to how they were secured to each other, and also the changing
  >'face' that they consequently offered the to the wind. I have
  >seen airborne plastic bags behave in a similar manner on a
  >number of occasions when at a similar altitude.
  I've seen a plastic bag blowing in the wind too, but the motion
  is typically random, maybe because it was opened at one end,
  allowing air to enter and leave and thus continually changing
  shape. The drawing shows a continuous sawtooth pattern, maybe
  more like a sailboat steering across the wind.
  Other very similar objects have appeared in different times and
  places as I pointed out. This strikes me as an unlikely
  coincidence if they were all balloons. For this reason, and the
  apparent morphing from one shape to another, and the observed
  non-random changes in direction, it is reasonable to suggest
  that it is something other than a set of balloons.

  William

  isolated case where such objects have appeared. This should have
  a bearing on the interpretation.

  is a balloon. In other circumstances, you might be accused of
  the sin of pareidolia - the "imagined perception of a pattern or
  meaning where it does not actually exist". Funny how the word
  never comes up unless the claim is considered extraordinary.
  >Out of interest, has the witness provided the video material?
  >That may provide a better insight as to whether or not the
  >object is or isn't a balloon cluster.

  video shows a different object meandering in and out of a cloud.
  Unfortunately, precisely where it gets interesting, there is a
  lot of camera movement. I'll try to deal with this by extracting
  a sequence of frames. The resolution is much lower of course.
  She also gave me more still shots of another object which I
  think is different again. I'm still working on how to present
  this stuff. Hopefully, you will be able to comment on these as
  well, soon.

  >As a person who has seen these triangular craft very up-close on

  staged false UFOs, for instance with planes flying in formation
  at night. I think it was mentioned as a possibility in the book
  by Imbrogno. The purpose would be to confuse the matter and open
  the door to plausible denial of real UFOs.
  Perhaps it is what happened in Phoenix. At 8 pm, a huge UFO
  passed over the city, as former governor Symington has now
  confirmed. But at 10 pm, there was a military exercise with
  flares above the mounains near the city.
  You see, an exercise in plausible deniability!
  And, remember, after the Hudson Valley sightings, there was the
  triangle wave in Belgium, and later in Great Britain which
  convinced Nick Pope of the reality of UFOs.

Re: An Unusual UFO Over Reading England

  The witness expectations have not been pointed in a particular
  direction, any more than anyone else who has seen something they
  don't understand. I certainly did not tell her what she saw. But
  maybe you think that was my first mistake - I should have told
  her it was nothing but a balloon.
  I was careful to not give an opinion on what the object was. I
  did suggest reasons why the obvious explanation of balloons
  might not be correct, and I drew comparisons with other cases. I
  presented what data I had, and left it up to readers to form
  their own opinion. Sometimes there is not enough information to
  come to a firm conclusion. Apparently a few did, and that's
  fine.

  William

  No, Don, this is a U.F.O. story.
  The NYT's treatment of the UFO phenomenon, so unrelentingly
  weird that it once inspired a JUFOS paper written by a couple of
  academics in media studies, is even now - more than 60 years in
  - akin to the behavior of someone in the vicinity of something
  giving off an offensive odor.
  Even today, it can't let go of those periods that separate "U"
  from "F" and "O" and, one infers, the NYT from an irritating fad
  which will fade away any moment now. The periods tell readers
  all they need to know.

  Jerry Clark

Secrecy News - 11/29/10


                                                          UFO Updates
                                   A mailing list for the study of UFO-related phenomena
                                               'Its All Here In Black & White'
    Location: UFOUpDatesList.Com > 2010 > Nov > Nov 29

                                                         Secrecy News - 11/29/10
  From: Steven Aftergood <saftergood.nul>
  Date: Mon, 29 Nov 2010 11:20:12 -0500
  Archived: Mon, 29 Nov 2010 13:22:04 -0500
  Subject: Secrecy News - 11/29/10

  SECRECY NEWS
  from the FAS Project on Government Secrecy
  Volume 2010, Issue No. 94
  November 29, 2010
  Secrecy News Blog: http://www.fas.org/blog/secrecy/

  **      THE RACE TO FIX THE CLASSIFICATION SYSTEM

  THE RACE TO FIX THE CLASSIFICATION SYSTEM
  The massive disclosure of a quarter million diplomatic records
  by Wikileaks this weekend underscores the precarious state of
  the U.S. national security classification system.
  The Wikileaks project seems to be, more than anything else, an
  assault on secrecy. If Wikileaks were most concerned about
  whistleblowing, it would focus on revealing corruption. If it
  were concerned with historical truth, it would emphasize the
  discovery of verifiably true facts. If it were anti-war, it
  would safeguard, not disrupt, the conduct of diplomatic
  communications. But instead, what Wikileaks has done is to
  publish a vast potpourri of records - dazzling, revelatory,
  true, questionable, embarrassing, or routine - whose only
  common feature is that they are classified or otherwise
  restricted.
  This may be understood as a reaction to a real problem, namely
  the fact that by all accounts, the scope of government secrecy
  in the U.S. (not to mention other countries) has exceeded
  rational boundaries. Disabling secrecy in the name of
  transparency would be a sensible goal - if it were true that
  all secrecy is wrong. But if there is a legitimate role for
  secrecy in military operations, in intelligence gathering or in
  diplomatic negotiations, as seems self-evident, then a different
  approach is called for.
  Although it has rarely been front-page news, important progress
  has been made this year in shifting U.S. government secrecy
  policy away from its cold war roots, and promoting greater
  discernment and discrimination in the use of national security
  classification.
  In May, the U.S. government formally disclosed the current size
  of the U.S. nuclear weapons arsenal for the first time (5,113
  warheads as of September 30, 2009). Declassification of this
  information, which is integral to future arms control and
  disarmament efforts, had been sought - and resisted - for
  decades. That battle for public disclosure has now been won.
  Also this year, the Report of the Nuclear Posture Review, the
  basic statement of U.S. nuclear weapons policy, was produced and
  released in unclassified form for the first time.
  In September, the Director of National Intelligence and the
  Secretary of Defense revealed the total intelligence budget
  ($80.1 billion in FY2010) as well as its "national" ($53.1
  billion) and military ($27 billion) components. This is a more


http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2010/nov/m29-001.shtml[12/12/2011 09:42:35]
Secrecy News - 11/29/10

  complete and detailed disclosure of U.S. intelligence spending
  than has ever been provided before. (An aggregate figure - with
  no further breakdown - was disclosed in 1997 and 1998.) It also
  represents a major policy reversal. Just a few years ago,
  intelligence community leaders swore under penalty of perjury
  that disclosure of this information would damage national
  security and compromise intelligence methods. Now annual
  intelligence budget disclosure is the new norm.
  These are not cosmetic changes. They represent real
  discontinuities with past practice. Stockpile secrecy and
  intelligence budget secrecy have each been cornerstones of
  entire edifices of national security classification that will
  now be susceptible to change. And in each case their disclosure
  is the culmination and the successful fruition of years or even
  decades of advocacy, agitation and litigation by the Federation
  of American Scientists and other organizations and political
  leaders.
  In fact, the deepest significance of these disclosures may lie
  in the fact that they demonstrate the feasibility of effective
  public advocacy in national security secrecy policy. If a half
  century of nuclear stockpile secrecy and intelligence budget
  secrecy can be overturned in favor of public disclosure, then
  citizens can confidently seek the release of many other, less
  deeply entrenched official secrets as well as a continuing
  reduction in the overall scope of the secrecy system.
  Of course, efforts to reduce government secrecy have not been
  uniformly successful. For example, the Obama Administration's
  use of the state secrets privilege to derail litigation on
  sensitive national security topics is indistinguishable from
  that of the Bush Administration, despite a September 2009 policy
  change promising "greater accountability" and more limited use
  of the privilege. Moreover, it appears that the Obama Justice
  Department has failed to fulfill its own policy of referring to
  agency Inspectors General any legitimate cases against the
  government that could not be litigated because of the state
  secrets privilege. (We are still attempting to confirm and to
  document that this is indeed the case.) Nor has it offered any
  other alternative remedy to those who may have been wronged by
  U.S. government actions concealed by state secrets claims.
  But even when the wheels of progress move slowly - or slip into
  reverse - proponents of greater openness are not helpless. At
  Secrecy News, we have tried to shine a spotlight on the
  mechanics of secrecy, and to provide our own almost daily
  disclosures of official documents of public policy value that
  are somehow restricted or otherwise hard to find. Not just
  because they are restricted, but because they are also of public
  policy value. Over the past year, Secrecy News produced unique
  coverage of numerous important secrecy stories. For example:
  ** Leaking classified information may be the right thing to do
  in certain circumstances, suggested district court Judge T.S.
  Ellis III at a 2009 hearing, "but you have to stand up and take
  the consequences." We obtained and released the previously
  unpublished transcript of that remarkable hearing last March.
  ("Judge: If You Leak Classified Info, Take the Consequences,"
  March 22).
  ** We offered the most complete and in-depth reporting of the
  dispute between Congress and the executive branch over
  Government Accountability Office access to intelligence
  information. We provided related documentation including a 1988
  Office of Legal Counsel opinion and a new Department of Defense
  instruction on GAO access to highly classified DoD special
  access programs. In congressional testimony and public advocacy,
  we also argued in favor of an increased role for GAO in
  intelligence oversight. Despite a veto threat from the White
  House earlier this year, a favorable resolution of the matter
  now seems to be within reach. ("GAO Gains a Foothold in
  Intelligence Oversight," September 29).
  ** We maintained and expanded our online library of reports from
  the JASON defense science advisory board. Ours is the most
  complete public collection of these consistently interesting and
  influential studies.
  ** We obtained and published numerous unreleased reports from
  the DNI Open Source Center, such as a March 2010 report on


http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2010/nov/m29-001.shtml[12/12/2011 09:42:35]
Secrecy News - 11/29/10

  Turkey's mysterious underground Ergenekon movement.
  ** We spent more time than we would have liked criticizing the
  Wikileaks organization, whose spectacular releases of large
  collections of classified documents continue to generate
  controversy. From our perspective, Wikileaks has been
  inattentive to the unintended consequences of its actions,
  careless about putting individuals in harm's way, particularly
  in the case of the Afghan war records, and ethically deficient
  in its invasions of personal privacy. (In its latest release,
  Wikileaks did redact some names of individuals and some other
  sensitive information.)
  ** With other like-minded organizations (and, in this case, a
  remarkably responsive White House), we helped prevent the
  creation of an ominous new information control system for so-
  called Controlled Unclassified Information. Instead of
  constituting a fourth level of classification, the new CUI
  marking should simply facilitate information sharing without
  providing authority for any new restrictions on information. ("A
  New Policy on Controlled Unclassified Info," November 4).
  ** We obtained and published a previously undisclosed 2009
  report from the Intelligence Science Board on the virtues of
  non-coercive interrogation. We also reported that the DNI had
  disbanded the ISB this year.
  ** We published hundreds of Congressional Research Service
  reports that had not previously been made available to the
  public, and numerous other popular records from a three-volume
  description of the Soviet army to the U.S. Army's latest weapons
  system handbook to a speculative scientific paper on
  "interstellar archeology." And quite a bit more.
  It's impossible to say whether the race to fix the
  classification system can be won through our kind of advocacy
  from the outside and by enlightened self-interest within
  government. Before that happens, classification itself could be
  rendered moot and ineffective by leaks, abuse or internal
  collapse. Or, in a reflexive response to continuing leaks,
  officials might seek to expand the scope of secrecy rather than
  focusing it narrowly, while increasing penalties for
  unauthorized disclosures.
  But in the coming year, we see some promise in what is called
  the Fundamental Classification Guidance Review. This is a
  procedure (mandated in executive order 13526, section 1.9) for
  every agency that classifies information to seek out, identify
  and remove classification requirements that are no longer valid.
  In effect, it provides an opportunity and a mechanism for
  rewriting the "software" of the entire classification system.
  Though success is not guaranteed, we expect the Review to
  produce a measurable reduction in the scope of national security
  classification. We plan to monitor its progress as closely as we
  can.
  Finally, we want to ask for your help. If you identify with our
  approach and you derive value from the work that we are doing,
  then we encourage you to help sustain it for another year with a
  tax-deductible contribution. Although we make our online
  resources freely available to everyone who wants them, we incur
  costs in collecting, analyzing, and publishing them as well as
  in our related advocacy activities. If you can help us with
  that, please do.
  Donations can be made online here (select "Government Secrecy"
  in the drop-down menu to allocate your donation for the FAS
  Project on Government Secrecy):
  http://www.fas.org/member/donate_today.html
  Donors who contribute $25 or more will automatically be enrolled
  as members of the Federation of American Scientists (unless you
  prefer not to be). Donations can also be made by sending a check
  made out to Federation of American Scientists and earmarked for
  Secrecy News to this address:
  Attn: Secrecy News
  Federation of American Scientists
  1725 DeSales Street NW, Suite 600
  Washington, DC 20036


http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2010/nov/m29-001.shtml[12/12/2011 09:42:35]

  >seem to have anything about the secret UFO projects, crashed
  >saucers, government contact with extraterrestrials and all the
  >other stuff that the government is supposed to be hiding about
  >UFOs
  I don't think it's curious at all.
  A cursory look says that the October release was entitled "The
  Iraq War Logs". The most recent releases are leaked United
  States embassy cables, and so far, only a small fraction of
  these have been released.
  According to
  http://cablegate.wikileaks.org/
  the most frequent subjects discussed in the embassy cables are:
  External political relations -- 145,451
  Internal government affairs -- 122,896
  Human rights -- 55,211
  Economic Conditions -- 49,044
  Terrorists and terrorism -- 28,801
  UN security council -- 6,532
  I would not expect to find conversations about top secret
  military technologies under these headings. I also would not
  expect to find confirmation of historical data such as the MJ-12
  documents, Roswell, etc. in recent embassy cables. Some
  discussion of potential ET space invaders might fit under one or
  two of these topics if that were seen internationally as a
  current threat.

  William

  >"Reek" is a statement of opinion. Of course I can't prove that.
  >But governments don't sit back and politely try to beg leakers of anything
  >_really_ serious to 'stop doing that.'
  >Especially governments with a history of thuggery to accomplish their
  ends.
  >That's the reason I hold that opinion.
  The person that apparently downloaded thousands of files onto a
  small USB memory stick is in custody and awaiting serious
  charges for his actions. But WikiLeaks is beyond US Government
  control and I suspect there are numerous efforts underway to try
  and shut it down, but so far there is no legal basis to stop the
  flow on their end. The public requests to cease and desist are
  only what you see on the surface.
  Unless there had been a sighting in the middle of the war that
  came to the attention of the military, this wouldn't contain any
  reference to alien craft or 'lights in the sky'.

  out. An example is given on page 119 of my Flying Saucers And
  Science. Only 8 words are not blacked out.

  Stan Friedman
